- **plexarm-chief-of-staff**: Sweeps the work record for what is late, unowned or unlinked, findings nobody acted on, and incidents with no write-up; reports the counts, fixes what is clerical and routes the rest to its owner. - **plexarm-data-analyst**: Answers a specific question with data: queries, analyses, dashboards, and funnel and cohort reads. - **plexarm-general-manager**: Use proactively for any job spanning more than one discipline: a page or feature built end to end, a launch, a change to personal data, anything touching sign-in, payments or permissions. Not for single-discipline work (renaming a variable, fixing one bug): use that specialist or do it inline. Agent 도구로 다른 subagent 를 직접 spawn 하는 오케스트레이터 모드를 갖는다. 키워드: PM, 일정, 진척, 작업 내역, 태스크 분해, 리스크, 블로커, 의존성, 회고, 스프린트, 스코프, 신규 서비스, 오케스트레이션. 요구사항은 planner, 구현은 developer, 테스트는 qa, 결정은 lead. ([secretj/secretj-claude-config](https://github.com/secretj/secretj-claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/pm.md))
- **pm**: Product Manager. Transforma pedidos em spec executável + contrato de API. Acione no início de qualquer feature, antes dos devs (ou depois do architect em features grandes). ([Sena-kj/ia-team-template](https://github.com/Sena-kj/ia-team-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pm.md))
- **pm**: Product-management role for discovery, product framing, milestones, and trade-offs. ([shaoster/glaze](https://github.com/shaoster/glaze/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pm.md))
